goals stay on the probes themselves; and passed rungs kept, ISL_KEEP_RUNGS=1: each generation one rung already passed is scored again in its own world and joins the selection cases]] with inhibit inputs [ISL_INHIBIT=1: a population may carry an inhibit input, a channel or port whose lines a frame ago are taken before its kernels fire, so a kernel whose mask lies more than half on them is held down; a new population has one one time in four] and the overlap rule as a gene [ISL_OVERLAP=1: a population's inhibit_quarters, 1 to 4 starting at 2, sets how much of a kernel's mask taken lines must cover to hold it down; 4 lets kernels on the same spot all fire] and steps up to the rungs no run has passed [ISL_STEPS=feel=100,glow=150,cycle1=100 and ISL_FEEL_GOAL=160: feel-100 before feel, whose goal is lowered from 300, out of reach, to 160, on switching's, shapes' and the maze's ladders, glow-150 before screen's glow, cycle1-100 before prediction's cycle1, the same worlds with lower goals] and easier reading rungs [ISL_MIRROR=1: echo2q, echo2m8 and echo2m4 before echo2, each token heard like the voice's code for it, first without one's own voice]] with size classes [ISL_SIZE_CLASSES=1: each island's genomes sorted by size into classes a factor of four apart, 1-3 tokens, 4-15, 16-63, 64-255 and 256+; each occupied class keeps its own lexicase choice among the elite, and half of all children have a parent chosen within a class drawn at random, so small and large networks both stay in the population]] with a wiring cost [ISL_WIRE_COST=1: each step a genome pays 1 for each bit of Hamming distance between the tags of the two ends of each of its populations, ports at fixed tags in the same space, as the carrying cost is paid; near ends cheap, far ones dear, so circuits that work together gather in one part of tag space near the ports they use], populations competing for their destination [ISL_COMPETE=1: the populations into a destination run strongest grip first, ties in genome order, and each word an earlier population's kernels wrote this step is claimed, and a kernel whose output words are mostly claimed is held down and learns anti-Hebbian, so the winners write the act, not the OR of every reflex] and pruning [ISL_PRUNE=1: each population holds its destination with its gene grip, 16 to start, up one a step it writes, down one a step it only loses, letting go for the lifetime at 0]) with branches (ISL_BRANCH=1: a population's fired kernels may also write their outputs into a second vector, a channel or an output port, at the same place by OR, as an axon's collaterals: an efference copy, a line to another population's inhibit input, two targets driven by one decision; a new population has one one time in four; under competition a branch writes only unclaimed words)) with sparse one-word codes on reading (ISL_ONE_WORD=8: every reading lifetime's glyphs, sounds and voice codes drawn as 8 random lines in the first word of their channels, so a glyph, its sound and its voice code sit at the same place and a word holding three codes stays readable) and a fixed body (ISL_BODY=1: the voice's and the hand's codes, the sound the voice makes for each token, which the teacher speaks too, and the feel of each move drawn from one seed for the whole run, as anatomy is, so evolution can build them in; glyphs, lights, tones, cues and what pays are still drawn fresh each lifetime, so learning is forced by the world)) with the three-factor output rule (ISL_ELIG=1: a kernel that fired k steps ago is checked while its population's gate, the modulator, is open, against what its destination holds then, less its own population's writes; right, it counts up; wrong, an output line moves toward what is there and it counts down, and wrong at 0 it dies, so its input is free for a new birth; a new population has it one time in four) and reading's paired-associate rungs (ISL_PAIRS=1: pairs8 and pairs32 after echo2q, glyph-to-token pairs drawn each lifetime, each token heard like its voice code, goal copy 100);
